- **chimes-active-learner**: Active-learning agent for ChIMES studies. Give it the study directory, the current model and data manifest, the target conditions and the labeling budget; it runs toolkit-managed active-learning rounds - MD harvest, fingerprint novelty, selection, QE labeling (submissions returned for approval), merging with a fixed holdout, refitting at the chosen hyperparameters with n/I weight decay, re-validation - and decides when to stop. Use to stabilize a model, when MD shows close contacts or instabilities, or for "improve this model with more data". ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-active-learner.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-benchmark**: Compute accounting and performance agent for ChIMES studies. Reports CPU-hours used across the development process (by phase and job, charged vs used), and plans, submits (with approval) and interprets strong/weak scaling benchmarks of the final model in LAMMPS, turning them into CPU-hour estimates for downstream production runs. Use for "how much compute did this take", "how does the model scale", "how much should I request for X atoms for Y ns". ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-benchmark.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-data-curator**: Data selection and curation agent for ChIMES studies. Give it the user's goal (elements, what the model is for, label target if known) and a study directory; it plans the dataset, searches open DFT databases, fetches or generates structures, curates them, and returns data_manifest.json plus the decisions that need the user (label source, QE submission). Use for the data phase of a ChIMES study or any "find/prepare training data for X" request. ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-data-curator.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-fit-reviewer**: Independent reviewer for a finished ChIMES fit - reads sweep tables, evaluate output, auto-build traces, fm_setup.in and params.txt, and says whether the model is trustworthy and what to try next. Use before recommending a model to the user, when a sweep or auto-build finishes, or when asked "is this fit any good". ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-fit-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-hyperparameter-tuner**: Hyperparameter selection agent for ChIMES studies. Give it a data_manifest.json (from the data phase), the study directory and the user's goal (what the model is for, MD cost limits, compute budget); it analyzes the data, plans and prepares the cutoff / Morse-lambda / polynomial-order search, interprets finished searches, and returns the chosen settings plus decisions and job submissions that need the user. Use for the fitting phase of a ChIMES study or any "which cutoffs/orders should I use" request. ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-hyperparameter-tuner.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-job-monitor**: Read-only status checker for long-running ChIMES work - Slurm jobs, DLARS solves, QE labeling batches, al_driver runs. Delegate to it when a job has been submitted and someone needs to know whether it is queued, running, finished or stuck, without spending the main conversation's context on squeue/sacct/log output. ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-job-monitor.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-md-validator**: MD validation agent for ChIMES studies. Give it candidate params.txt files (or a finished hyper-search), the study directory and the conditions the model must handle; it plans and prepares md-check runs (stability, close contacts, RDF vs DFT), compares MD configurations with the training data by cluster-graph fingerprint, and returns a verdict - which model to use, or that active learning is needed and which frames to label. Use after a fit or search, when choosing between tied models, or for "is this model stable in MD". ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-md-validator.md) · ★1)
- **chimes-report-writer**: Final-report agent for ChIMES studies. Collates everything a study produced - data selection and curation, hyperparameter search, final model, active learning, MD runs, scaling benchmark, CPU-hours, deployed model card - and writes a human-readable REPORT.md whose every number traces to the study's artifacts. Use when a study is complete (or paused) and a write-up is needed. ([laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES](https://github.com/laubachb/Agentic-ChIMES/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chimes-report-writer.md) · ★1)

- **chip-lowpower-designer**: 芯片低功耗设计 Agent。规划功耗域划分和电压域定义，生成 IEEE 1801 UPF 约束文件，设计 Isolation/Level Shifter 方案，制定 Clock Gating（ICG）插入策略，输出动态/静态功耗分析报告。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统（预编译结构化知识），功耗方案可参考工艺库漏电参数和标准单元特性。遵循编码规范（coding-style.md）确保 ICG 实例化符合项目标准。遵循 DDD 领域驱动设计规范，功耗域划分可参考 FS §4.5 领域模型的 Aggregate 边界。当用户需要进行功耗域规划、UPF 编写、低功耗方案设计或功耗分析时激活。触发词：'低功耗'、'UPF'、'功耗域'、'clock gating'、'power'、'isolation'、'功耗分析'。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-lowpower-designer.md) · ★31)

- **chip-project-lead**: 芯片项目总负责人。把控从需求到交付的全流程质量门控，识别和管控技术/进度/接口/需求/资源五类风险，跟踪关键路径和里程碑进度，协调跨模块接口对齐和资源冲突。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统（预编译结构化知识），方案评审时可检索协议规范和 CBB 选型指南。遵循 SDD 规格驱动追溯规范，可追踪 REQ 覆盖率和全链路进度。遵循编码规范（coding-style.md）理解 RTL 实现约束。当用户需要项目管理、风险评估、进度跟踪、跨模块协调或门控检查时激活。触发词：'项目管理'、'风险评估'、'进度跟踪'、'汇报'、'协调'、'门控'、'里程碑'。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-project-lead.md) · ★31)
- **chip-qpcr-analyzer**: Use this agent when you have ChIP-qPCR data in triplicate format that needs outlier filtering, percentage input enrichment calculation, and publication-quality bar plot generation. The assay is H3K27ac ChIP-qPCR in response to Drug X."\nassistant: "I'll use the chip-qpcr-analyzer agent to process y… ([hdbp/claude](https://github.com/hdbp/claude/blob/HEAD/claude-code/agents/chip-qpcr-analyzer.md))

- **chip-sta-analyst**: 芯片综合与时序分析 Agent。使用 Yosys 进行 RTL 逻辑综合，编写 SDC 时序约束，分析关键路径和时序违例，输出面积预估报告。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统（预编译结构化知识），综合约束可参考协议规范和工艺库参数。遵循编码规范（coding-style.md）确保约束与 RTL 一致。遵循 SDD 规格驱动追溯规范，综合约束可追溯到 FS PPA 指标。当用户需要执行综合、编写 SDC 约束、分析时序违例或预估面积时激活。触发词：'综合'、'时序分析'、'SDC'、'面积预估'、'timing'、'synthesis'、'lint'、'时序违例'。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-sta-analyst.md) · ★31)
- **chip-sw-driver**: 芯片硬件驱动架构规划 Agent。根据 FS 寄存器表和 RTL 寄存器模块，规划驱动分层架构、定义 API 接口、设计寄存器映射方案、输出驱动架构文档。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统（预编译结构化知识），协议驱动架构设计时可检索 NVMe/Virtio/RDMA/SPDK/DPDK 等协议知识。集成对抗性评审（devils-advocate balanced 模式），可在驱动架构文档完成后自动挑战设计假设。不参与代码编写，代码实现由 chip-firmware-writer 负责。当用户需要规划驱动架构、设计 API 接口或定义寄存器映射方案时激活。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-sw-driver.md) · ★31)
- **chip-sw-profiler**: 芯片软件性能分析 Agent。对固件代码进行延迟分析、吞吐测量、CPU 占用分析和优化建议。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统，支持 Bare-metal/Linux/RTOS 多平台性能分析。当用户需要分析驱动性能、定位瓶颈或优化吞吐时激活。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-sw-profiler.md) · ★31)
- **chip-sw-reviewer**: 芯片软件代码审查与驱动架构评审 Agent。对固件代码进行静态分析、安全审查、编码规范检查；对驱动架构文档进行分层合理性、API 一致性、寄存器映射、中断/DMA 架构、多平台适配评审。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统，支持 Linux kernel coding style 和安全编码规范。当用户需要审查驱动代码、进行静态分析、检查编码规范或评审驱动架构文档时激活。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-sw-reviewer.md) · ★31)
- **chip-sw-verifier**: 芯片软件验证 Agent。根据驱动架构文档和固件代码，生成测试计划、单元测试、集成测试、Mock 硬件层和覆盖率报告。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统，支持 Bare-metal/Linux/RTOS 多平台测试。当用户需要编写驱动测试、生成测试计划或分析测试覆盖率时激活。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-sw-verifier.md) · ★31)
- **chip-top-integrator**: 芯片顶层集成 Agent。收集子模块端口定义，检查跨模块接口一致性（命名/位宽/方向/协议），编写顶层模块（仅实例化+连线，禁止逻辑），运行系统级 Lint，生成模块连接拓扑图。内置 LLM Wiki 知识系统（预编译结构化知识），集成时可检索协议规范确认接口合规性。遵循 SDD 规格驱动追溯规范，集成时验证接口信号与 FS/UA 定义的一致性。遵循编码规范（coding-style.md）确保连线符合项目标准。当用户需要进行子模块集成、接口对齐、顶层连线或系统级检查时激活。触发词：'顶层集成'、'接口对齐'、'系统lint'、'连线'、'top integration'、'connect'。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-top-integrator.md) · ★31)
- **chip-verfi-arch**: 芯片验证架构 Agent。根据 FS 功能规格书、UA 微架构文档和 BDD 行为场景文档，生成测试点分解、验证环境方案与用例规划。支持 BDD 场景驱动的测试点分解（Given→前置条件，When→激励，Then→检查）和 DDD 领域驱动的验证策略（Entity→身份追踪，Aggregate→原子性验证，Domain Event→事件序列覆盖），遵循 SDD 规格驱动追溯规范。总验证方案输出到 `dv/doc/plan/`，测试点与用例规划输出到 `dv/doc/check_point/`，各组件详细实现方案输出到 `dv/env/plan/`。不负责生成 TB 代码和验证环境代码，专注于验证策略的顶层设计。集成对抗性评审（debate plan 模式），可在验证方案生成后自动触发跨模型评审。当用户需要制定验证计划、分解测试点或规划验证环境方案时激活。 ([zhaixin244-wq/fnw](https://github.com/zhaixin244-wq/fnw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chip-verfi-arch.md) · ★31)

- **chittyagent-serv**: Use this agent for the ongoing care of the chittyserv compute fleet — the portable tower of Mac Mini and Dell micro nodes plus chittyserv-vm and chittyclaw. - **chizen-figures**: Reviews a paper's figures and tables against the CHI Zen style guide - show the data, high data-ink ratio, legible in black and white, labelled axes with units, self-contained captions, CHI Zen palettes, booktabs tables, \Description alt text, no subfigures. Give it the paper directory (and optionally figure/table files or labels). Read-only. ([JimWallace/CHI-Zen](https://github.com/JimWallace/CHI-Zen/blob/HEAD/plugins/chizen/agents/chizen-figures.md) · ★2)
- **chizen-references**: Reviews an HCI paper's citations and bibliography against the CHI Zen style guide - in-text citation practice (the checklist's YES/NO table), every claim cited, entries verified against authoritative records (DOI/Crossref, DBLP, ACM DL), DOIs present, no duplicates or preprints standing in for published versions, BibTeX with ACM-Reference-Format, TAPS-safe packages for camera-ready. - **chizen-statistics**: Reviews the quantitative reporting of an HCI paper against the CHI Zen style guide - alpha stated, sample size justified, the right test for the design, assumptions, multiple-comparison corrections, effect sizes and confidence intervals, precision via the stats.tex macros (F to 1 decimal, p to 2 with &lt; .001), no "trending toward significance", Likert data treated as ordinal. Use for any bug report against the desktop app where the cause isn't already obvious from a stack trace or test failure — not for pure feature work. ([TareqAmeer/Chroma-App](https://github.com/TareqAmeer/Chroma-App/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chromasmith-debugger.md) · ★68)
